Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on math_logic structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 54. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 649 with relations in degree 2.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the math_logic decomposition. By minimality, the element a^54 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 649-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
